++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n  return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n   printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfeZrTpAFe7SkSSd6hLxj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015MjPLqH3eyHvz7r1rHswSr","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_hybrid_r1/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA Cahn-Hilliard benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization:\n//  - MPI: 1D slab decomposition along z. Each rank owns planes [z0, z1) and keeps\n//    two ghost planes on each side for c (so that mu can be computed one plane\n//    beyond the owned range and only one halo exchange per time step is needed).\n//  - CUDA: one GPU per rank (selected by node-local rank); stencil kernels run on\n//    the device. Halo exchange is overlapped with the interior update.\n//  - OpenMP: host-side work (initialization, pack/unpack of gathered output,\n//    validation reductions).\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                   \\\n    do {                                                                                   \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                         \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                             \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                  \\\n        }                                                                                  \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Clamped 7-point Laplacian. 'p' points at the center value; sx/sy/sz are the\n// (possibly zero, due to clamping) offsets to the +/- neighbours.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ p, const ptrdiff_t xm, const ptrdiff_t xp,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t ym, const ptrdiff_t yp, const ptrdiff_t zm,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t zp, const double c2, const double idx2,\n                                            const double idy2, const double idz2) {\n    const double cxx = (__ldg(p + xp) + __ldg(p - xm) - 2.0 * c2) * idx2;\n    const double cyy = (__ldg(p + yp) + __ldg(p - ym) - 2.0 * c2) * idy2;\n    const double czz = (__ldg(p + zp) + __ldg(p - zm) - 2.0 * c2) * idz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\nstruct StencilParams {\n    int nx, ny, nz;   // global grid\n    int zlo;          // global z index of local plane 0\n    double idx2, idy2, idz2;\n};\n\n// mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on global planes [zb, ze)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                                const StencilParams P, const int zb,\n                                                                const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                                                const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = c + idx;\n    const double cv = __ldg(p);\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n              gamma * laplacian(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, cv, P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                     const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams P,\n                                                     const int zb, const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = mu + idx;\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    cnew[idx] = __ldg(cold + idx) + dtD * laplacian(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, __ldg(p), P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (global planes [zb, ze) into local buffer starting at plane zlo)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zb, const size_t ze, const size_t zlo) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = zb; z < ze; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z - zlo, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nstatic inline dim3 planeGrid(const dim3& block, int nx, int ny, int nplanes) {\n    return dim3((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y, nplanes);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    int worldRank = 0, worldSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n    const bool root = (worldRank == 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if Each rank owns planes [z0, z1) and keeps\n//    two ghost planes on each side for c (so that mu can be computed one plane\n//    beyond the owned range and only one halo exchange per time step is needed).\n//  - CUDA: one GPU per rank (selected by node-local rank); stencil kernels run on\n//    the device. Halo exchange is overlapped with the interior update.\n//  - OpenMP: host-side work (initialization, pack/unpack of gathered output,\n//    validation reductions).\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                   \\\n    do {                                                                                   \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                         \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                             \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                  \\\n        }                                                                                  \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Clamped 7-point Laplacian. 'p' points at the center value; sx/sy/sz are the\n// (possibly zero, due to clamping) offsets to the +/- neighbours.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ p, const ptrdiff_t xm, const ptrdiff_t xp,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t ym, const ptrdiff_t yp, const ptrdiff_t zm,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t zp, const double c2, const double idx2,\n                                            const double idy2, const double idz2) {\n    const double cxx = (__ldg(p + xp) + __ldg(p - xm) - 2.0 * c2) * idx2;\n    const double cyy = (__ldg(p + yp) + __ldg(p - ym) - 2.0 * c2) * idy2;\n    const double czz = (__ldg(p + zp) + __ldg(p - zm) - 2.0 * c2) * idz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\nstruct StencilParams {\n    int nx, ny, nz;   // global grid\n    int zlo;          // global z index of local plane 0\n    double idx2, idy2, idz2;\n};\n\n// mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on global planes [zb, ze)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                                const StencilParams P, const int zb,\n                                                                const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                                                const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = c + idx;\n    const double cv = __ldg(p);\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n              gamma * laplacian(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, cv, P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                     const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams P,\n                                                     const int zb, const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = mu + idx;\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    cnew[idx] = __ldg(cold + idx) + dtD * laplacian(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, __ldg(p), P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (global planes [zb, ze) into local buffer starting at plane zlo)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zb, const size_t ze, const size_t zlo) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = zb; z < ze; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z - zlo, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n Each rank owns planes [z0, z1) and keeps\n//    two ghost planes on each side for c (so that mu can be computed one plane\n//    beyond the owned range and only one halo exchange per time step is needed).\n//  - CUDA: one GPU per rank (selected by node-local rank); stencil kernels run on\n//    the device. Halo exchange is overlapped with the interior update.\n//  - OpenMP: host-side work (initialization, pack/unpack of gathered output,\n//    validation reductions).\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                   \\\n    do {                                                                                   \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                         \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                             \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                  \\\n        }                                                                                  \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Clamped 7-point Laplacian. 'p' points at the center value; xm/xp/... are the\n// (possibly zero, due to clamping) offsets to the -/+ neighbours. With unit grid\n// spacing the (exact) multiplications by 1/(d*d) == 1.0 are skipped, which matters\n// because the kernels are FP64-throughput bound on many GPUs.\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ p, const ptrdiff_t xm, const ptrdiff_t xp,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t ym, const ptrdiff_t yp, const ptrdiff_t zm,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t zp, const double cv, const double idx2,\n                                            const double idy2, const double idz2) {\n    const double c2 = 2.0 * cv;\n    double cxx = __ldg(p + xp) + __ldg(p - xm) - c2;\n    double cyy = __ldg(p + yp) + __ldg(p - ym) - c2;\n    double czz = __ldg(p + zp) + __ldg(p - zm) - c2;\n    if constexpr (!UnitSpacing) {\n        cxx *= idx2;\n        cyy *= idy2;\n        czz *= idz2;\n    }\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\nstruct StencilParams {\n    int nx, ny, nz;   // global grid\n    int zlo;          // global z index of local plane 0\n    double idx2, idy2, idz2;\n};\n\n// mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z).\n// The bulk term 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 is linear in c\n// apart from c^3 and is evaluated as fmaA*c + fmaB + c^3 (coefficients from the host).\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                                const StencilParams P, const int zb,\n                                                                const double gamma, const double bulkA,\n                                                                const double bulkB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = c + idx;\n    const double cv = __ldg(p);\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    mu[idx] = (bulkA * cv + bulkB) + cv * cv * cv -\n              gamma * laplacian<UnitSpacing>(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, cv, P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z)\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                     const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams P,\n                                                     const int zb, const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = mu + idx;\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    cnew[idx] = __ldg(cold + idx) + dtD * laplacian<UnitSpacing>(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, __ldg(p), P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (global planes [zb, ze) into local buffer starting at plane zlo)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zb, const size_t ze, const size_t zlo) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = zb; z < ze; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z - zlo, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; 'p' points at the center value; xm/xp/... are the\n// (possibly zero, due to clamping) offsets to the -/+ neighbours. With unit grid\n// spacing the (exact) multiplications by 1/(d*d) == 1.0 are skipped, which matters\n// because the kernels are FP64-throughput bound on many GPUs.\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ p, const ptrdiff_t xm, const ptrdiff_t xp,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t ym, const ptrdiff_t yp, const ptrdiff_t zm,\n                                            const ptrdiff_t zp, const double cv, const double idx2,\n                                            const double idy2, const double idz2) {\n    const double c2 = 2.0 * cv;\n    double cxx = __ldg(p + xp) + __ldg(p - xm) - c2;\n    double cyy = __ldg(p + yp) + __ldg(p - ym) - c2;\n    double czz = __ldg(p + zp) + __ldg(p - zm) - c2;\n    if constexpr (!UnitSpacing) {\n        cxx *= idx2;\n        cyy *= idy2;\n        czz *= idz2;\n    }\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\nstruct StencilParams {\n    int nx, ny, nz;   // global grid\n    int zlo;          // global z index of local plane 0\n    double idx2, idy2, idz2;\n};\n\n// mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z).\n// The bulk term 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 is linear in c\n// apart from c^3 and is evaluated as fmaA*c + fmaB + c^3 (coefficients from the host).\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                                const StencilParams P, const int zb,\n                                                                const double gamma, const double bulkA,\n                                                                const double bulkB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = c + idx;\n    const double cv = __ldg(p);\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    mu[idx] = (bulkA * cv + bulkB) + cv * cv * cv -\n              gamma * laplacian<UnitSpacing>(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, cv, P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu) on global planes [zb, zb + gridDim.z)\ntemplate <bool UnitSpacing>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                     const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams P,\n                                                     const int zb, const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = zb + blockIdx.z;\n    if (x >= P.nx || y >= P.ny) return;\n    const ptrdiff_t plane = (ptrdiff_t)P.nx * P.ny;\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)(z - P.zlo) * plane + (size_t)y * P.nx + x;\n    const double* p = mu + idx;\n    const ptrdiff_t xp = (x < P.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0, xm = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t yp = (y < P.ny - 1) ? P.nx : 0, ym = (y > 0) ? P.nx : 0;\n    const ptrdiff_t zp = (z < P.nz - 1) ? plane : 0, zm = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n    cnew[idx] = __ldg(cold + idx) + dtD * laplacian<UnitSpacing>(p, xm, xp, ym, yp, zm, zp, __ldg(p), P.idx2, P.idy2, P.idz2);\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (global planes [zb, ze) into local buffer starting at plane zlo)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zb, const size_t ze, const size_t zlo) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = zb; z < ze; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z - zlo, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfeaqFt9cybR8zdtfveuj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The benchmark now runs on MPI + OpenMP + CUDA. Four RTX 3090s reach about 39 GCell/s at 512³, against 0.16 GCell/s for the original serial code, which is about 94% parallel efficiency. Results match the serial version to rounding error (~1e-16 relative), not bit-for-bit. Only `cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` changed, and the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`.\n\n**How it's split up**\n- **MPI:** the grid is cut into slabs along z, one per rank, with at least 2 planes each; any extra ranks sit idle. Each rank keeps 2 halo planes per side, so only one halo exchange is needed per time step. The final field is gathered onto rank 0 only when `-r` or `-v` is given.\n- **CUDA:** each rank uses one GPU, chosen by its rank within the node. Each step runs two kernels: chemical potential, then the concentration update. They use the same clamped-boundary 7-point stencil as before.\n- **Overlap:** the boundary planes are computed first and sent while the GPU works on the interior. The parts of the next step that don't need neighbour data also run during the exchange. This OpenMPI build is not CUDA-aware, so halos go through pinned host memory.\n- **OpenMP:** host-side initialisation and the validation checks.\n- **Build:** `CMakeLists.txt` now finds MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA toolkit, and compiles the existing `.cpp` file with nvcc. It targets the local GPU architecture by default; you can override it with `-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES`.\n\n**Speed-ups that change rounding slightly**\nThe 3090's double-precision units are the bottleneck, so I cut floating-point work in two ways:\n- With unit grid spacing (the benchmark's setting), the divisions by dx² are skipped. Dividing by 1.0 is exact, so this changes nothing.\n- The bulk term `4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c·e_AB) + 3c + c³` is rewritten as `A·c + B + c³`. A and B are computed once from the same parameters. This is mathematically identical but rounds differently at the last bit.\n\n**Correctness checks**\n- **Rank count:** the output hash is identical for 1, 2, 3, 4 and 6 ranks, on several cube and odd-shaped grids, including ones with only 5 or 9 z-planes.\n- **Against the serial build:** min, max and samples agree to about 1e-16 relative, and `-v` passes. The hash itself differs, as expected: the original build's own result already depends on how the compiler fuses multiply-adds (`-ffp-contract=off` changes it). Exact hash matching against the serial binary won't work, so compare with a tolerance.\n- **Bug found and fixed:** the first version of the overlapped exchange had a race that gave wrong results on 4 ranks. It is fixed, and the checks above were run after the fix.\n\n**Performance at 512³ (GCell/s)**\n\n| GPUs | Speed |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | ~10.4 |\n| 2 | ~20.5 |\n| 4 | ~39 |\n\nI tried two other options and left both out:\n- **Merging the two kernels into one:** this would recompute work at the tile edges, which is a poor trade when double-precision throughput is the limit, as on the 3090.\n- **A cheaper Laplacian formula:** it was 12% faster on one GPU but only about 1% on four, and it rounds less like the original.\n\nOn GPUs with strong double-precision units (A100/H100), memory bandwidth would be the limit instead, and merging the kernels could pay off there."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":430,"cache_read_input_tokens":61299,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":430},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e6b39378-849c-4928-818d-9b54bb888434","uuid":"d904caeb-3f7e-48d3-91f5-01593d8a03d6","timestamp":"2026-10-03T03:21:18.239Z","request_id":"req_011CfeaqFa3QxjQ7EAuzvShG"}
